# Plan: Bezirks-Heatmap zur Auswertung hinzufügen (Issue #13)

## Context
GitHub issue #13 requests a Bezirk heatmap on the Auswertung page: "Österreich Karte mit allen Bezirken und dann auf einer Skala von grün bis rot Heatmap". The pie chart stays (user override). The existing Bundesland map also stays — the Bezirk heatmap is an **addition**.

## Approach: Static SVG paths (matching existing AustriaMap pattern)

The existing `AustriaMap.tsx` uses inline SVG paths from MapSVG for 9 Bundesländer. We follow the same pattern for ~94 Bezirke:

1. **Source**: Download Bezirke GeoJSON from `github.com/ginseng666/GeoJSON-TopoJSON-Austria` (99.5% simplified, CC BY-SA 3.0)
2. **Convert**: One-time Node script projects GeoJSON coordinates to SVG paths matching the existing map's coordinate system
3. **Embed**: Store as `client/src/data/bezirkPaths.ts` — a static TypeScript file (~200-300KB source, ~50-80KB gzipped)
4. **Render**: New `AustriaBezirkMap.tsx` component using same SVG pattern as `AustriaMap.tsx`

No new runtime dependencies. Zero D3. Consistent with existing codebase.

## Steps

### 1. Generate Bezirk SVG path data
- Create `scripts/generate-bezirk-paths.mjs` — one-time script
- Download GeoJSON, project WGS84 → SVG coordinates (calibrated to existing map viewBox)
- Map 3-digit ISO Gemeindekennzahl codes → 2-letter KFZ Bezirk codes (hardcoded mapping table, ~94 entries from `seed.ts`)
- Output: `client/src/data/bezirkPaths.ts`
  ```ts
  export const BEZIRK_PATHS: Record<string, {
    d: string; labelX: number; labelY: number;
    name: string; bundesland_code: string;
  }> = { ... };
  ```
- Calibrate projection using reference points from existing BL_PATHS (Wien center ~550,97 = 16.37°E,48.21°N)
- Wien = single polygon (merge 23 sub-districts if needed)

### 2. Create `client/src/components/AustriaBezirkMap.tsx`
- Props: `{ data: { bezirk_code: string; participants: number; reports: number }[] }`
- Color: green→yellow→red HSL gradient (H: 120→0) — distinct from the blue Bundesland map
- Gray (#f3f4f6) for Bezirke with 0 participants
- Bezirk borders: white, 0.5px stroke
- Bundesland borders overlaid: #1e3a5f, 1.5px stroke (reuse BL_PATHS from AustriaMap)
- Tooltip on hover: Bezirk name, code, Bundesland, participants, reports
- Legend at bottom (green→red gradient bar)
- Labels: show Bezirk code only for Bezirke with data (avoid clutter)
- ViewBox: tight to Austria only (no neighbor countries)

### 3. Integrate into `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`
- Import `AustriaBezirkMap`
- Add `bezirkMapCardRef` for PNG export
- Place **after** the existing Austria Map section (~line 423), before the charts
- Only show when `stats.bezirkStats.length > 0`
- Wire up download button with existing `downloadChart()` function
- Feed data: `stats.bezirkStats.map(bz => ({ bezirk_code: bz.bezirk_code, participants: bz.participants, reports: bz.reports }))`

## Files

| File | Action | Notes |
|------|--------|-------|
| `scripts/generate-bezirk-paths.mjs` | CREATE | One-time GeoJSON→SVG conversion script |
| `client/src/data/bezirkPaths.ts` | CREATE | Generated static SVG path data for ~94 Bezirke |
| `client/src/components/AustriaBezirkMap.tsx` | CREATE | New heatmap component |
| `client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx` | MODIFY | Add bezirk heatmap section |

## Key References
- `client/src/components/AustriaMap.tsx` — existing pattern to follow (SVG paths, tooltip, legend, color gradient)
- `client/src/types/stats.ts` — `BezirkStat` interface (data shape)
- `server/src/db/seed.ts` — complete list of 94 Bezirke with KFZ codes
- Existing map viewBox: `"-300 -120 1100 560"`

## Verification
1. `nix-shell -p nodejs --run "npm run build"` compiles clean
2. Auswertung page shows Bezirk heatmap below Bundesland map
3. Bezirke with participants colored green→red, empty ones gray
4. Hover tooltip shows correct Bezirk name + stats
5. PNG download works via existing mechanism
6. Pie chart still visible (not removed)
7. Deploy via `./deploy-production.sh`

    39→  const date = new Date(d + 'T00:00:00');
    40→  return date.toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
    41→}
    42→
    43→function toISO(d: Date) {
    44→  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    45→}
    46→
    47→export default function AggregatedReportsPage() {
    48→  const now = new Date();
    49→  const year = now.getFullYear();
    50→
    51→  const [from, setFrom] = useState(() => toISO(new Date(year, 0, 1)));
    52→  const [to, setTo] = useState(() => toISO(new Date(year, 11, 31)));
    53→  const [data, setData] = useState<AggregatedResponse | null>(null);
    54→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    55→  const [showExercises, setShowExercises] = useState(false);
    56→  const [showParticipants, setShowParticipants] = useState(true);
    57→  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false);
    58→  const [selectedTypes, setSelectedTypes] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());

    66→
    67→  const fetchStats = (f: string, t: string, types?: Set<string>) => {
    68→    setLoading(true);
    69→    const typesParam = types && types.size > 0 ? `&types=${Array.from(types).map(encodeURIComponent).join(',')}` : '';
    70→    apiFetch(`/api/v1/reports/stats?from=${f}&to=${t}${typesParam}`)
    71→      .then((res) => setData(res))
    72→      .catch(() => setData(null))
    73→      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    74→  };
    75→
    76→  useEffect(() => {
    77→    fetchStats(from, to, selectedTypes);
    78→  }, []);
    79→
    80→  const applyRange = (f: string, t: string) => {
    81→    setFrom(f);

    86→  const applyQuarter = (q: number) => {

    88→    const end = new Date(year, q * 3, 0);

    91→
    92→  const applyYear = () => {

   183→      link.href = url;
   184→      link.download = `BOS-ARSA_Auswertung_${from}_${to}.pdf`;
   185→      link.click();
   186→      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
   187→    } catch {} finally {
   188→      setExporting(false);
   189→    }
   190→  };
   191→
   192→  const stats = data?.stats ?? null;
   193→  const exercises = data?.exercises ?? [];
   194→
   195→  // Group bezirk stats by Bundesland
   196→  const byBl: Record<string, { name: string; bezirke: BezirkStat[]; totalParticipants: number; totalReports: number }> = {};
   197→  if (stats) {
   198→    for (const bz of stats.bezirkStats) {
   199→      if (!byBl[bz.bundesland_code]) {
   200→        byBl[bz.bundesland_code] = { name: bz.bundesland, bezirke: [], totalParticipants: 0, totalReports: 0 };
   201→      }
   202→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code].bezirke.push(bz);
   203→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalParticipants += bz.participants;
   204→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalReports += bz.reports;
   205→    }
   206→  }
   207→
   208→  const avgParticipants = exercises.length > 0
   209→    ? Math.round((stats?.totalParticipants ?? 0) / exercises.length * 10) / 10
   210→    : 0;
   211→
   212→  return (
   213→    <div ref={pageRef} className="space-y-6">
   214→      {/* Header */}
   215→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
   216→        <div className="min-w-0">
   217→          <h1 className="text-base sm:text-xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">Auswertung</h1>
   218→          {exercises.length > 0 && (
   219→            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500">
   220→              {formatDateDE(from)} – {formatDateDE(to)} · {exercises.length} Übung{exercises.length !== 1 ? 'en' : ''}
   221→            </p>
   222→          )}
   223→        </div>
   224→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-shrink-0" data-no-export="true">
   225→          {stats && exercises.length > 0 && (
   226→            <>
   227→              <button
   228→                onClick={async () => {
   229→                  try {
   230→                    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
   231→                    const res = await fetch(`/api/v1/reports/adif?from=${from}&to=${to}`, {
   232→                      headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},
   233→                    });
   234→                    if (!res.ok) throw new Error();
   235→                    const blob = await res.blob();
   236→                    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
   237→                    const link = document.createElement('a');
   238→                    link.href = url;
   239→                    link.download = `BOS-ARSA_QSOs_${from}_${to}.adi`;
   240→                    link.click();
   241→                    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
   242→                  } catch {}
   243→                }}
   244→                className="bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 text-white px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs sm:text-sm"
   245→                title="ADIF-Datei für QRZ.com, LOTW etc."
   246→              >
   247→                Download ADIF
   248→              </button>
   249→              <button
   250→                onClick={downloadFullPage}
   251→                disabled={exporting}
   252→                className="bg-[#1e3a5f] hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] text-white px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs sm:text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
   253→              >
   254→                {exporting ? '...' : 'Download Auswertung'}
   255→              </button>
   256→            </>
   257→          )}
   258→        </div>
   259→      </div>
   260→
   261→      {/* Date range selector */}
   262→      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-3 sm:p-4" data-no-export="true">
   263→        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-3">
   264→          {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((q) => (
   265→            <button
   266→              key={q}
   267→              onClick={() => applyQuarter(q)}
   268→              className="bg-gray-100 hover:bg-blue-100 text-gray-700 hover:text-[#1e3a5f] px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   269→            >
   270→              Q{q}
   271→            </button>
   272→          ))}
   273→          <button
   274→            onClick={applyYear}
   275→            className="bg-gray-100 hover:bg-blue-100 text-gray-700 hover:text-[#1e3a5f] px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   276→          >
   277→            Jahr {year}
   278→          </button>
   279→        </div>
   280→        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm">
   281→          <label className="text-gray-500 text-xs">Von</label>
   282→          <input
   283→            type="date"

   286→            className="border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm"
   287→          />
   288→          <label className="text-gray-500 text-xs">Bis</label>

   307→            </label>

   325→        </div>

   619→                  </thead>
   620→                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   621→                    {stats.participants.map((p, idx) => (
   622→                      <tr key={p.callsign} className="hover:bg-blue-50">
   623→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-xs text-gray-400">{idx + 1}</td>
   624→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 font-mono font-medium">{p.callsign}{p.suffixes ? p.suffixes.split(',').filter(Boolean).map(s => s.startsWith('/') ? s : `/${s}`).join('') : ''}</td>
   625→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 hidden sm:table-cell">{p.name || ''}</td>
   626→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1">
   627→                          {p.bezirk_code && (
   628→                            <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono">{p.bezirk_code}</span>
   629→                          )}
   630→                        </td>
   631→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 text-xs hidden md:table-cell">{p.bundesland_name || ''}</td>
   632→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-right font-mono">{p.report_count}</td>
   633→                      </tr>
   634→                    ))}
   635→                  </tbody>
   636→                </table>
   637→              </div>
   638→            )}
   639→          </div>
   640→
   641→          {/* Export section */}
   642→          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4" data-no-export="true">
   643→            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f] mb-3">Export</h2>
   644→            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
   645→              <button
   646→                onClick={downloadFullPage}
   647→                disabled={exporting}
   648→                className="bg-[#1e3a5f] hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium disabled:opacity-50"
   649→              >
   650→                {exporting ? 'Exportieren...' : 'Download Auswertung'}
   651→              </button>
   652→              <button
   653→                onClick={async () => {
   654→                  try {
   655→                    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
   656→                    const res = await fetch(`/api/v1/reports/adif?from=${from}&to=${to}`, {
   657→                      headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},
   658→                    });
   659→                    if (!res.ok) throw new Error();
   660→                    const blob = await res.blob();
   661→                    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
   662→                    const link = document.createElement('a');
   663→                    link.href = url;
   664→                    link.download = `BOS-ARSA_QSOs_${from}_${to}.adi`;
   665→                    link.click();
   666→                    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
   667→                  } catch {}
   668→                }}
   669→                className="bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   670→              >
   671→                Download ADIF
   672→              </button>
   673→            </div>
   674→            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-2">
   675→              ADIF-Datei kann in QRZ.com, LOTW oder andere Logbuch-Software importiert werden.
   676→            </p>
   677→          </div>
   678→        </>
   679→      )}
   680→    </div>
   681→  );
   682→}

   148→function getColor(value: number, max: number): string {
   149→  if (max === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
   150→  if (value === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
   151→  const t = value / max;
   152→  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);
   153→  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);
   154→  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);
   155→  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
   156→}
   157→
   158→export default function AustriaMap({ data }: Props) {
   159→  const [tooltip, setTooltip] = useState<{
   160→    x: number;
   161→    y: number;
   162→    code: string;
   163→    name: string;
   164→    participants: number;
   165→    reports: number;
   166→  } | null>(null);
   167→
   168→  const dataMap = new Map(data.map((d) => [d.code, d]));
   169→  const maxParticipants = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.participants), 1);
   170→
   171→  const showTooltip = (e: React.MouseEvent, code: string, labelX: number, labelY: number, participants: number, reports: number) => {
   172→    const svg = e.currentTarget.closest('svg');
   173→    if (!svg) return;
   174→    const rect = svg.getBoundingClientRect();
   175→    const pt = svg.createSVGPoint();
   176→    pt.x = labelX;
   177→    pt.y = labelY;
   178→    const ctm = svg.getScreenCTM();
   179→    if (!ctm) return;
   180→    const screenPt = pt.matrixTransform(ctm);
   181→    setTooltip({
   182→      x: screenPt.x - rect.left,
   183→      y: screenPt.y - rect.top - 10,
   184→      code: BL_SHORT[code] || code,
   185→      name: BL_NAMES[code] || code,
   186→      participants,
   187→      reports,
   188→    });
   189→  };
   190→
   191→  return (
   192→    <div className="relative">
   193→      <svg
   194→        viewBox="-300 -120 1100 560"
   195→        className="w-full h-auto"
   196→        role="img"
   197→        aria-label="Karte von Österreich und Nachbarländer mit Teilnehmern"
   198→      >
   199→        {/* Clip to visible area */}
   200→        <defs>
   201→          <clipPath id="map-clip">
   202→            <rect x="-300" y="-120" width="1100" height="560" />
   203→          </clipPath>
   204→        </defs>
   205→
   206→        {/* Background: neighbor country borders */}
   207→        <g clipPath="url(#map-clip)">
   208→          {Object.entries(NEIGHBOR_PATHS).map(([code, { d, labelX, labelY }]) => {
   209→            const bl = dataMap.get(code);
   210→            const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;
   211→            const reports = bl?.reports ?? 0;
   212→            const hasPeople = participants > 0;
   213→            const fill = hasPeople ? getColor(participants, maxParticipants) : '#e5e7eb';
   214→
   215→            return (
   216→              <g
   217→                key={code}
   218→                onMouseEnter={(e) => showTooltip(e, code, labelX, labelY, participants, reports)}
   219→                onMouseLeave={() => setTooltip(null)}
   220→                className={hasPeople ? 'cursor-pointer' : ''}
   221→              >
   222→                <path
   223→                  d={d}
   224→                  fill={fill}
   225→                  stroke="#d1d5db"
   226→                  strokeWidth="1"
   227→                  opacity={hasPeople ? 0.9 : 0.6}
   228→                  className="transition-opacity hover:opacity-80"
   229→                />
   230→                {hasPeople && (
   231→                  <>
   232→                    <text
   233→                      x={labelX}
   234→                      y={labelY - 5}
   235→                      textAnchor="middle"
   236→                      fontSize="12"
   237→                      fontWeight="bold"
   238→                      fill="#1e3a5f"
   239→                      pointerEvents="none"
   240→                    >
   241→                      {BL_SHORT[code]}
   242→                    </text>
   243→                    <text
   244→                      x={labelX}
   245→                      y={labelY + 9}
   246→                      textAnchor="middle"
   247→                      fontSize="10"
   248→                      fill="#1e3a5f"
   249→                      pointerEvents="none"
   250→                    >
   251→                      {participants}
   252→                    </text>
   253→                  </>
   254→                )}
   255→                {!hasPeople && (
   256→                  <text
   257→                    x={labelX}
   258→                    y={labelY}
   259→                    textAnchor="middle"
   260→                    fontSize="11"
   261→                    fill="#9ca3af"
   262→                    pointerEvents="none"
   263→                  >
   264→                    {BL_SHORT[code]}
   265→                  </text>
   266→                )}
   267→              </g>
   268→            );
   269→          })}
   270→        </g>
   271→
   272→        {/* Austrian Bundesländer (foreground, on top of neighbors) */}
   273→        {Object.entries(BL_PATHS).map(([code, { d, labelX, labelY }]) => {
   274→          const bl = dataMap.get(code);
   275→          const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;
   276→          const reports = bl?.reports ?? 0;
   277→          const fill = getColor(participants, maxParticipants);
   278→          const textColor = participants / maxParticipants > 0.5 ? '#ffffff' : '#1e3a5f';
   279→
   280→          return (
   281→            <g
   282→              key={code}
   283→              onMouseEnter={(e) => showTooltip(e, code, labelX, labelY, participants, reports)}
   284→              onMouseLeave={() => setTooltip(null)}
   285→              className="cursor-pointer"
   286→            >
   287→              <path
   288→                d={d}
   289→                fill={fill}
   290→                stroke="#ffffff"
   291→                strokeWidth="2"
   292→                className="transition-opacity hover:opacity-80"
   293→              />
   294→              <text
   295→                x={labelX}

   298→                fontSize="10"

   300→                fill={textColor}
   301→                pointerEvents="none"
   302→              >

   305→              <text
   306→                x={labelX}

   308→                textAnchor="middle"
   309→                fontSize="9"
   310→                fill={textColor}
   311→                pointerEvents="none"
   312→              >
   313→                {participants}
   314→              </text>
   315→            </g>
   316→          );
   317→        })}
   318→      </svg>

   320→      {/* Tooltip */}

   324→          style={{

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env node\n/**\n * One-time script: download Austrian Bezirke GeoJSON and convert to SVG paths.\n * Output: client/src/data/bezirkPaths.ts\n *\n * Projection calibrated to match existing AustriaMap.tsx BL_PATHS coordinate system.\n * Source: github.com/ginseng666/GeoJSON-TopoJSON-Austria (CC BY-SA 3.0)\n */\n\nimport { writeFileSync } from 'fs';\nimport { resolve, dirname } from 'path';\nimport { fileURLToPath } from 'url';\n\nconst __dirname = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\n\n// GeoJSON URL — 99.5% simplified bezirke\nconst GEOJSON_URL = ───\n// Calibration: Wien center in existing SVG ≈ (550, 97)\n// Wien real coords ≈ (16.37°E, 48.21°N)\n// Vorarlberg center in existing SVG ≈ (30, 215)\n// Bregenz real coords ≈ (9.75°E, 47.50°N)\n//\n// From these two reference points:\n// x: 550 = 16.37 * sx + ox  AND  30 = 9.75 * sx + ox\n//   → sx = (550 - 30) / (16.37 - 9.75) = 520 / 6.62 ≈ 78.55\n//   → ox = 550 - 16.37 * 78.55 ≈ 550 - 1285.45 ≈ -735.45\n//\n// y: 97 = 48.21 * sy + oy  AND  215 = 47.50 * sy + oy  (note: lat increases = y decreases)\n//   → sy = (97 - 215) / (48.21 - 47.50) = -118 / 0.71 ≈ -166.20\n//   → oy = 97 - 48.21 * (-166.20) ≈ 97 + 8014.3 ≈ 8111.3\n\nconst SX = 78.55;\nconst OX = -735.45;\nconst SY = -166.20;\nconst OY = 8111.3;\n\nfunction projectLon(lon) { return lon * SX + OX; }\nfunction projectLat(lat) { return lat * SY + OY; }\n\nfunction coordsToSvgPath(rings) {\n  return rings.map((ring, ri) => {\n    const parts = ring.map(([lon, lat], i) => {\n      const x = projectLon(lon);\n      const y = projectLat(lat);\n      const xr = Math.round(x * 100) / 100;\n      const yr = Math.round(y * 100) / 100;\n      return `${i === 0 ? 'M' : 'L'}${xr},${yr}`;\n    });\n    return parts.join('') + 'Z';\n  }).join('');\n}\n\nfunction getCentroid(rings) {\n  // Use first (outer) ring\n  const ring = rings[0];\n  let cx = 0, cy = 0;\n  for (const [lon, lat] of ring) {\n    cx += projectLon(lon);\n    cy += projectLat(lat);\n  }\n  return { x: Math.round(cx / ring.length * 100) / 100, y: Math.round(cy / ring.length * 100) / 100 };\n}\n\nasync function main() {\n  console.log('Fetching GeoJSON...');\n  const response = await fetch(GEOJSON_URL);\n  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);\n  const geojson = await response.json();\n  console.log(`Got ${geojson.features.length} features`);\n\n  // Group features by KFZ code (Wien 23 sub-districts → merge)\n  const byKfz = {};\n\n  for (const feature of geojson.features) {\n    const props = feature.properties;\n    // The GeoJSON uses 'iso' or 'id' for the district code\n    const gkz = String(props.iso || props.id || '');\n    const kfz = GKZ_TO_KFZ[gkz];\n    if (!kfz) {\n      console.warn(`  Unknown GKZ: ${gkz} (${props.name})`);\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (!byKfz[kfz]) byKfz[kfz] = [];\n\n    const geom = feature.geometry;\n    if (geom.type === 'Polygon') {\n      byKfz[kfz].push(geom.coordinates);\n    } else if (geom.type === 'MultiPolygon') {\n      for (const polygon of geom.coordinates) {\n        byKfz[kfz].push(polygon);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  console.log(`Mapped to ${Object.keys(byKfz).length} Bezirke`);\n\n  // Build output\n  const entries = {};\n  for (const [kfz, polygons] of Object.entries(byKfz)) {\n    const meta = BEZIRK_META[kfz];\n    if (!meta) {\n      console.warn(`  No metadata for KFZ: ${kfz}`);\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    // Merge all rings into a single SVG path\n    const allRings = [];\n    const allOuterRings = [];\n    for (const polygon of polygons) {\n      for (const ring of polygon) {\n        allRings.push(ring);\n      }\n      allOuterRings.push(polygon[0]); // outer ring for centroid\n    }\n\n    const d = coordsToSvgPath(allRings);\n\n    // Centroid from all outer rings combined\n    let cx = 0, cy = 0, n = 0;\n    for (const ring of allOuterRings) {\n      for (const [lon, lat] of ring) {\n        cx += projectLon(lon);\n        cy += projectLat(lat);\n        n++;\n      }\n    }\n    cx = Math.round(cx / n * 100) / 100;\n    cy = Math.round(cy / n * 100) / 100;\n\n    entries[kfz] = {\n      d,\n      labelX: cx,\n      labelY: cy,\n      name: meta.name,\n      bundesland_code: meta.bundesland_code,\n    };\n  }\n\n  // Generate TypeScript output\n  const lines = [\n    '// Auto-generated by scripts/generate-bezirk-paths.mjs',\n    '// Source: github.com/ginseng666/GeoJSON-TopoJSON-Austria (CC BY-SA 3.0)',\n    '// Do not edit manually.',\n    '',\n    'export const BEZIRK_PATHS: Record<string, {',\n    '  d: string; labelX: number; labelY: number;',\n    '  name: string; bundesland_code: string;',\n    '}> = {',\n  ];\n\n  const sortedKeys = Object.keys(entries).sort((a, b) => {\n    const ba = entries[a].bundesland_code;\n    const bb = entries[b].bundesland_code;\n    if (ba !== bb) return ba.localeCompare(bb);\n    return a.localeCompare(b);\n  });\n\n  for (const kfz of sortedKeys) {\n    const e = entries[kfz];\n    lines.push(`  '${kfz}': {`);\n    lines.push(`    d: '${e.d}',`);\n    lines.push(`    labelX: ${e.labelX}, labelY: ${e.labelY},`);\n    lines.push(`    name: '${e.name.replace(/'/g, \"\\\\'\")}', bundesland_code: '${e.bundesland_code}',`);\n    lines.push(`  },`);\n  }\n\n  lines.push('};');\n  lines.push('');\n\n  const outPath = resolve(__dirname, '../client/src/data/bezirkPaths.ts');\n  writeFileSync(outPath, lines.join('\\n'));\n  console.log(`Written ${sortedKeys.length} Bezirke to ${outPath}`);\n  console.log(`File size: ${(lines.join('\\n').length / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`);\n}\n\nmain().catch(console.error);\n"
}

BL_NAMES: Record<string, string> = {\n  '01': 'Wien', '02': 'Salzburg', '03': 'Niederösterreich', '04': 'Burgenland',\n  '05': 'Oberösterreich', '06': 'Steiermark', '07': 'Tirol', '08': 'Kärnten',\n  '09': 'Vorarlberg',\n};\n\nfunction getHeatColor(value: number, max: number): string {\n  if (max === 0 || value === 0) return '#f3f4f6';\n  const t = Math.min(value / max, 1);\n  // Green (120) → Yellow (60) → Red (0) via HSL\n  const h = 120 * (1 - t);\n  const s = 70 + 15 * t; // 70-85%\n  const l = 45 + 10 * (1 - t); // 45-55%\n  return `hsl(${h}, ${s}%, ${l}%)`;\n}\n\nexport default function AustriaBezirkMap({ data }: Props) {\n  const [tooltip, setTooltip] = useState<{\n    x: number;\n    y: number;\n    code: string;\n    name: string;\n    bundesland: string;\n    participants: number;\n    reports: number;\n  } | null>(null);\n\n  const dataMap = new Map(data.map((d) => [d.bezirk_code, d]));\n  const maxParticipants = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.participants), 1);\n\n  const showTooltip = (\n    e: React.MouseEvent,\n    code: string,\n    labelX: number,\n    labelY: number,\n    participants: number,\n    reports: number,\n    name: string,\n    bundesland: string,\n  ) => {\n    const svg = e.currentTarget.closest('svg');\n    if (!svg) return;\n    const rect = svg.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const pt = svg.createSVGPoint();\n    pt.x = labelX;\n    pt.y = labelY;\n    const ctm = svg.getScreenCTM();\n    if (!ctm) return;\n    const screenPt = pt.matrixTransform(ctm);\n    setTooltip({\n      x: screenPt.x - rect.left,\n      y: screenPt.y - rect.top - 10,\n      code,\n      name,\n      bundesland,\n      participants,\n      reports,\n    });\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"relative\">\n      <svg\n        viewBox=\"-10 -10 640 320\"\n        className=\"w-full h-auto\"\n        role=\"img\"\n        aria-label=\"Bezirks-Heatmap von Österreich\"\n      >\n        {/* Bezirk polygons */}\n        {Object.entries(BEZIRK_PATHS).map(([code, { d, labelX, labelY, name, bundesland_code }]) => {\n          const bz = dataMap.get(code);\n          const participants = bz?.participants ?? 0;\n          const reports = bz?.reports ?? 0;\n          const fill = getHeatColor(participants, maxParticipants);\n          const blName = BL_NAMES[bundesland_code] || '';\n\n          return (\n            <path\n              key={code}\n              d={d}\n              fill={fill}\n              stroke=\"#ffffff\"\n              strokeWidth=\"0.5\"\n              className=\"transition-opacity hover:opacity-75 cursor-pointer\"\n              onMouseEnter={(e) => showTooltip(e, code, labelX, labelY, participants, reports, name, blName)}\n              onMouseLeave={() => setTooltip(null)}\n            />\n          );\n        })}\n\n        {/* Bundesland borders overlay */}\n        {Object.values(BL_BORDER_PATHS).map((d, i) => (\n          <path\n            key={`bl-${i}`}\n            d={d}\n            fill=\"none\"\n            stroke=\"#1e3a5f\"\n            strokeWidth=\"1.5\"\n            pointerEvents=\"none\"\n          />\n        ))}\n\n        {/* Labels for Bezirke with data */}\n        {Object.entries(BEZIRK_PATHS).map(([code, { labelX, labelY }]) => {\n          const bz = dataMap.get(code);\n          if (!bz || bz.participants === 0) return null;\n          return (\n            <text\n              key={`label-${code}`}\n              x={labelX}\n              y={labelY}\n              textAnchor=\"middle\"\n              dominantBaseline=\"central\"\n              fontSize=\"6\"\n              fontWeight=\"bold\"\n              fill=\"#1e3a5f\"\n              pointerEvents=\"none\"\n              stroke=\"#ffffff\"\n              strokeWidth=\"2\"\n              paintOrder=\"stroke\"\n            >\n              {code}\n            </text>\n          );\n        })}\n      </svg>\n\n      {/* Tooltip */}\n      {tooltip && (\n        <div\n          className=\"absolute bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded px-2 py-1.5 pointer-events-none shadow-lg z-10 whitespace-nowrap\"\n          style={{\n            left: tooltip.x,\n            top: tooltip.y,\n            transform: 'translate(-50%, -100%)',\n          }}\n        >\n          <div className=\"font-bold\">{tooltip.code} — {tooltip.name}</div>\n          <div className=\"text-gray-300\">{tooltip.bundesland}</div>\n          <div>Teilnehmer: {tooltip.participants}</div>\n          <div>Rapporte: {tooltip.reports}</div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Legend */}\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500\">\n        <span>0</span>\n        <div\n          className=\"h-3 w-32 rounded\"\n          style={{\n            background: 'linear-gradient(to right, hsl(120,70%,55%), hsl(60,78%,50%), hsl(0,85%,45%))',\n          }}\n        />\n        <span>{maxParticipants}</span>\n        <span className=\"ml-1 text-gray-400\">Teilnehmer</span>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

All tasks complete. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

**Bezirks-Heatmap (Issue #13)** — 4 files changed/created:

1. **`scripts/generate-bezirk-paths.mjs`** — One-time script that downloads Austrian Bezirke GeoJSON and converts to SVG paths, calibrated to match the existing AustriaMap coordinate system. Maps 3-digit GKZ codes to KFZ Bezirk codes.

2. **`client/src/data/bezirkPaths.ts`** — Generated static data file with SVG paths for all 94 Bezirke (130KB source, ~50KB gzipped). Includes path data, label coordinates, name, and bundesland_code.

3. **`client/src/components/AustriaBezirkMap.tsx`** — New heatmap component:
   - Green→Yellow→Red HSL gradient (distinct from the blue Bundesland map)
   - Gray (`#f3f4f6`) for Bezirke with 0 participants
   - White 0.5px Bezirk borders, navy 1.5px Bundesland border overlay
   - Hover tooltip showing Bezirk name, code, Bundesland, participants, reports
   - Labels (Bezirk code) shown only for Bezirke with data, with white stroke halo for readability
   - Legend bar at bottom

4. **`client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`** — Added the Bezirk heatmap section after the existing Bundesland map, with PNG download support. Only shown when `bezirkStats.length > 0`. Existing pie chart and Bundesland map are preserved.

```
   215→  'KL': { name: 'Klagenfurt-Land', bundesland_code: '08' },
   216→  'SV': { name: 'Sankt Veit an der Glan', bundesland_code: '08' },
   217→  'SP': { name: 'Spittal an der Drau', bundesland_code: '08' },
   218→  'VI': { name: 'Villach Stadt', bundesland_code: '08' },
   219→  'VL': { name: 'Villach-Land', bundesland_code: '08' },
   220→  'VK': { name: 'Völkermarkt', bundesland_code: '08' },
   221→  'WO': { name: 'Wolfsberg', bundesland_code: '08' },
   222→  'BZ': { name: 'Bludenz', bundesland_code: '09' },
   223→  'BC': { name: 'Bregenz', bundesland_code: '09' },
   224→  'DO': { name: 'Dornbirn', bundesland_code: '09' },
   225→  'FK': { name: 'Feldkirch', bundesland_code: '09' },
   226→};
   227→
   228→// ─── Projection ───
   229→// Calibration: Wien center in existing SVG ≈ (550, 97)
   230→// Wien real coords ≈ (16.37°E, 48.21°N)
   231→// Vorarlberg center in existing SVG ≈ (30, 215)
   232→// Bregenz real coords ≈ (9.75°E, 47.50°N)
   233→//
   234→// From these two reference points:
   235→// x: 550 = 16.37 * sx + ox  AND  30 = 9.75 * sx + ox
   236→//   → sx = (550 - 30) / (16.37 - 9.75) = 520 / 6.62 ≈ 78.55
   237→//   → ox = 550 - 16.37 * 78.55 ≈ 550 - 1285.45 ≈ -735.45
   238→//
   239→// y: 97 = 48.21 * sy + oy  AND  215 = 47.50 * sy + oy  (note: lat increases = y decreases)
   240→//   → sy = (97 - 215) / (48.21 - 47.50) = -118 / 0.71 ≈ -166.20
   241→//   → oy = 97 - 48.21 * (-166.20) ≈ 97 + 8014.3 ≈ 8111.3
   242→
   243→const SX = 78.55;
   244→const OX = -735.45;
   245→const SY = -166.20;
   246→const OY = 8111.3;
   247→
   248→function projectLon(lon) { return lon * SX + OX; }
   249→function projectLat(lat) { return lat * SY + OY; }
   250→
   251→function coordsToSvgPath(rings) {
   252→  return rings.map((ring, ri) => {
   253→    const parts = ring.map(([lon, lat], i) => {
   254→      const x = projectLon(lon);
   255→      const y = projectLat(lat);
   256→      const xr = Math.round(x * 100) / 100;
   257→      const yr = Math.round(y * 100) / 100;
   258→      return `${i === 0 ? 'M' : 'L'}${xr},${yr}`;
   259→    });
   260→    return parts.join('') + 'Z';
   261→  }).join('');
   262→}
   263→
   264→function getCentroid(rings) {
   265→  // Use first (outer) ring
   266→  const ring = rings[0];
   267→  let cx = 0, cy = 0;
   268→  for (const [lon, lat] of ring) {
   269→    cx += projectLon(lon);
   270→    cy += projectLat(lat);
   271→  }
   272→  return { x: Math.round(cx / ring.length * 100) / 100, y: Math.round(cy / ring.length * 100) / 100 };
   273→}
   274→

   274→
   275→async function main() {
   276→  console.log('Fetching GeoJSON...');
   277→  const response = await fetch(GEOJSON_URL);
   278→  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
   279→  const geojson = await response.json();
   280→  console.log(`Got ${geojson.features.length} features`);
   281→
   282→  // Group features by KFZ code (Wien 23 sub-districts → merge)
   283→  const byKfz = {};
   284→
   285→  for (const feature of geojson.features) {
   286→    const props = feature.properties;
   287→    // The GeoJSON uses 'iso' or 'id' for the district code
   288→    const gkz = String(props.iso || props.id || '');
   289→    const kfz = GKZ_TO_KFZ[gkz];
   290→    if (!kfz) {
   291→      console.warn(`  Unknown GKZ: ${gkz} (${props.name})`);
   292→      continue;
   293→    }
   294→    if (!byKfz[kfz]) byKfz[kfz] = [];
   295→
   296→    const geom = feature.geometry;
   297→    if (geom.type === 'Polygon') {
   298→      byKfz[kfz].push(geom.coordinates);
   299→    } else if (geom.type === 'MultiPolygon') {
   300→      for (const polygon of geom.coordinates) {
   301→        byKfz[kfz].push(polygon);
   302→      }
   303→    }
   304→  }
   305→
   306→  console.log(`Mapped to ${Object.keys(byKfz).length} Bezirke`);
   307→
   308→  // Build output
   309→  const entries = {};
   310→  for (const [kfz, polygons] of Object.entries(byKfz)) {
   311→    const meta = BEZIRK_META[kfz];
   312→    if (!meta) {
   313→      console.warn(`  No metadata for KFZ: ${kfz}`);
   314→      continue;
   315→    }
   316→
   317→    // Merge all rings into a single SVG path
   318→    const allRings = [];
   319→    const allOuterRings = [];
   320→    for (const polygon of polygons) {
   321→      for (const ring of polygon) {
   322→        allRings.push(ring);
   323→      }
   324→      allOuterRings.push(polygon[0]); // outer ring for centroid
   325→    }
   326→
   327→    const d = coordsToSvgPath(allRings);
   328→
   329→    // Centroid from all outer rings combined
   330→    let cx = 0, cy = 0, n = 0;
   331→    for (const ring of allOuterRings) {
   332→      for (const [lon, lat] of ring) {
   333→        cx += projectLon(lon);
   334→        cy += projectLat(lat);
   335→        n++;
   336→      }
   337→    }
   338→    cx = Math.round(cx / n * 100) / 100;
   339→    cy = Math.round(cy / n * 100) / 100;
   340→
   341→    entries[kfz] = {
   342→      d,
   343→      labelX: cx,
   344→      labelY: cy,
   345→      name: meta.name,
   346→      bundesland_code: meta.bundesland_code,
   347→    };
   348→  }
   349→
   350→  // Generate TypeScript output
   351→  const lines = [
   352→    '// Auto-generated by scripts/generate-bezirk-paths.mjs',
   353→    '// Source: github.com/ginseng666/GeoJSON-TopoJSON-Austria (CC BY-SA 3.0)',
   354→    '// Do not edit manually.',
   355→    '',
   356→    'export const BEZIRK_PATHS: Record<string, {',
   357→    '  d: string; labelX: number; labelY: number;',
   358→    '  name: string; bundesland_code: string;',
   359→    '}> = {',
   360→  ];
   361→
   362→  const sortedKeys = Object.keys(entries).sort((a, b) => {
   363→    const ba = entries[a].bundesland_code;
   364→    const bb = entries[b].bundesland_code;
   365→    if (ba !== bb) return ba.localeCompare(bb);
   366→    return a.localeCompare(b);
   367→  });
   368→
   369→  for (const kfz of sortedKeys) {
   370→    const e = entries[kfz];
   371→    lines.push(`  '${kfz}': {`);
   372→    lines.push(`    d: '${e.d}',`);
   373→    lines.push(`    labelX: ${e.labelX}, labelY: ${e.labelY},`);
   374→    lines.push(`    name: '${e.name.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', bundesland_code: '${e.bundesland_code}',`);
   375→    lines.push(`  },`);
   376→  }
   377→
   378→  lines.push('};');
   379→  lines.push('');
   380→
   381→  const outPath = resolve(__dirname, '../client/src/data/bezirkPaths.ts');
   382→  writeFileSync(outPath, lines.join('\n'));
   383→  console.log(`Written ${sortedKeys.length} Bezirke to ${outPath}`);
   384→  console.log(`File size: ${(lines.join('\n').length / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`);
   385→}
   386→
   387→main().catch(console.error);
   388→

}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/scripts/generate-bezirk-paths.mjs",
  "old_string": "  lines.push('};');\n  lines.push('');\n\n  const outPath = resolve(__dirname, '../client/src/data/bezirkPaths.ts');\n  writeFileSync(outPath, lines.join('\\n'));\n  console.log(`Written ${sortedKeys.length} Bezirke to ${outPath}`);\n  console.log(`File size: ${(lines.join('\\n').length / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`);\n}",
  "new_string": "  lines.push('};');\n  lines.push('');\n\n  // ─── Bundesländer borders (same projection) ───\n  const blEntries = {};\n  for (const feature of blGeojson.features) {\n    const props = feature.properties;\n    const iso = String(props.iso || props.id || '');\n    const dbCode = BL_ISO_TO_DB[iso];\n    if (!dbCode) {\n      console.warn(`  Unknown BL iso: ${iso} (${props.name})`);\n      continue;\n    }\n    const geom = feature.geometry;\n    const allRings = [];\n    if (geom.type === 'Polygon') {\n      for (const ring of geom.coordinates) allRings.push(ring);\n    } else if (geom.type === 'MultiPolygon') {\n      for (const polygon of geom.coordinates) {\n        for (const ring of polygon) allRings.push(ring);\n      }\n    }\n    blEntries[dbCode] = coordsToSvgPath(allRings);\n  }\n\n  lines.push('export const BL_BORDER_PATHS: Record<string, string> = {');\n  for (const code of Object.keys(blEntries).sort()) {\n    lines.push(`  '${code}': '${blEntries[code]}',`);\n  }\n  lines.push('};');\n  lines.push('');\n\n  const outPath = resolve(__dirname, '../client/src/data/bezirkPaths.ts');\n  writeFileSync(outPath, lines.join('\\n'));\n  console.log(`Written ${sortedKeys.length} Bezirke + ${Object.keys(blEntries).length} BL borders to ${outPath}`);\n  console.log(`File size: ${(lines.join('\\n').length / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`);\n}"
}

{

  "old_string": "// ─── Projection ───\n// Calibration: Wien center in existing SVG ≈ (550, 97)\n// Wien real coords ≈ (16.37°E, 48.21°N)\n// Vorarlberg center in existing SVG ≈ (30, 215)\n// Bregenz real coords ≈ (9.75°E, 47.50°N)\n//\n// From these two reference points:\n// x: 550 = 16.37 * sx + ox  AND  30 = 9.75 * sx + ox\n//   → sx = (550 - 30) / (16.37 - 9.75) = 520 / 6.62 ≈ 78.55\n//   → ox = 550 - 16.37 * 78.55 ≈ 550 - 1285.45 ≈ -735.45\n//\n// y: 97 = 48.21 * sy + oy  AND  215 = 47.50 * sy + oy  (note: lat increases = y decreases)\n//   → sy = (97 - 215) / (48.21 - 47.50) = -118 / 0.71 ≈ -166.20\n//   → oy = 97 - 48.21 * (-166.20) ≈ 97 + 8014.3 ≈ 8111.3\n\nconst SX = 78.55;\nconst OX = -735.45;\nconst SY = -166.20;\nconst OY = 8111.3;\n\nfunction projectLon(lon) { return lon * SX + OX; }\nfunction projectLat(lat) { return lat * SY + OY; }",
  "new_string": "// ─── Mercator Projection ───\n// The existing AustriaMap BL_PATHS use Mercator projection (from MapSVG).\n// We must use Mercator Y to match the aspect ratio.\n//\n// Mercator Y formula: y_merc = ln(tan(π/4 + lat_rad/2))\n//\n// Calibration from existing BL_PATHS reference points:\n//   Wien:     16.37°E, 48.21°N → SVG (550, 97)\n//   Bregenz:   9.75°E, 47.50°N → SVG (30, 215)\n//\n// X is linear: x = lon * SX + OX\n//   SX = (550 - 30) / (16.37 - 9.75) = 78.55\n//   OX = 550 - 16.37 * 78.55 = -735.45\n//\n// Y uses Mercator: y = mercY(lat) * SY_M + OY_M\n//   mercY(48.21°) = ln(tan(π/4 + 0.4207)) = 1.1178\n//   mercY(47.50°) = ln(tan(π/4 + 0.4145)) = 1.0972\n//   SY_M = (97 - 215) / (1.1178 - 1.0972) = -118 / 0.02063 = -5721.8\n//   OY_M = 97 - 1.1178 * (-5721.8) = 97 + 6396.0 = 6493.0\n\nconst SX = 78.55;\nconst OX = -735.45;\nconst SY_M = -5721.8;\nconst OY_M = 6493.0;\n\nfunction mercatorY(latDeg) {\n  const latRad = latDeg * Math.PI / 180;\n  return Math.log(Math.tan(Math.PI / 4 + latRad / 2));\n}\n\nfunction projectLon(lon) { return lon * SX + OX; }\nfunction projectLat(lat) { return mercatorY(lat) * SY_M + OY_M; }"

   241→//   OX = 550 - 16.37 * 78.55 = -735.45
   242→//
   243→// Y uses Mercator: y = mercY(lat) * SY_M + OY_M
   244→//   mercY(48.21°) = ln(tan(π/4 + 0.4207)) = 1.1178
   245→//   mercY(47.50°) = ln(tan(π/4 + 0.4145)) = 1.0972
   246→//   SY_M = (97 - 215) / (1.1178 - 1.0972) = -118 / 0.02063 = -5721.8
   247→//   OY_M = 97 - 1.1178 * (-5721.8) = 97 + 6396.0 = 6493.0
   248→
   249→const SX = 78.55;
   250→const OX = -735.45;
   251→const SY_M = -5721.8;
   252→const OY_M = 6493.0;
   253→
   254→function mercatorY(latDeg) {
   255→  const latRad = latDeg * Math.PI / 180;
   256→  return Math.log(Math.tan(Math.PI / 4 + latRad / 2));
   257→}
   258→
   259→function projectLon(lon) { return lon * SX + OX; }
   260→function projectLat(lat) { return mercatorY(lat) * SY_M + OY_M; }
   261→
   262→function coordsToSvgPath(rings) {
   263→  return rings.map((ring, ri) => {
   264→    const parts = ring.map(([lon, lat], i) => {
   265→      const x = projectLon(lon);
   266→      const y = projectLat(lat);
   267→      const xr = Math.round(x * 100) / 100;
   268→      const yr = Math.round(y * 100) / 100;
   269→      return `${i === 0 ? 'M' : 'L'}${xr},${yr}`;
   270→    });
   271→    return parts.join('') + 'Z';
   272→  }).join('');
   273→}
   274→
   275→function getCentroid(rings) {
   276→  // Use first (outer) ring
   277→  const ring = rings[0];
   278→  let cx = 0, cy = 0;
   279→  for (const [lon, lat] of ring) {
   280→    cx += projectLon(lon);
   281→    cy += projectLat(lat);
   282→  }
   283→  return { x: Math.round(cx / ring.length * 100) / 100, y: Math.round(cy / ring.length * 100) / 100 };
   284→}
   285→
   286→// Bundesland iso code → DB code
   287→const BL_ISO_TO_DB = {
   288→  '1': '04', // Burgenland
   289→  '2': '08', // Kärnten
   290→  '3': '03', // Niederösterreich
   291→  '4': '05', // Oberösterreich
   292→  '5': '02', // Salzburg
   293→  '6': '06', // Steiermark
   294→  '7': '07', // Tirol
   295→  '8': '09', // Vorarlberg
   296→  '9': '01', // Wien
   297→};
   298→
   299→async function main() {
   300→  console.log('Fetching Bezirke GeoJSON...');
   301→  const response = await fetch(BEZIRKE_URL);
   302→  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
   303→  const geojson = await response.json();
   304→  console.log(`Got ${geojson.features.length} bezirk features`);
   305→
   306→  console.log('Fetching Bundesländer GeoJSON...');
   307→  const blResponse = await fetch(LAENDER_URL);
   308→  if (!blResponse.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${blResponse.status}`);
   309→  const blGeojson = await blResponse.json();
   310→  console.log(`Got ${blGeojson.features.length} bundesland features`);
   311→
   312→  // Group features by KFZ code (Wien 23 sub-districts → merge)
   313→  const byKfz = {};
   314→
   315→  for (const feature of geojson.features) {
   316→    const props = feature.properties;
   317→    // The GeoJSON uses 'iso' or 'id' for the district code
   318→    const gkz = String(props.iso || props.id || '');
   319→    const kfz = GKZ_TO_KFZ[gkz];
   320→    if (!kfz) {
   321→      console.warn(`  Unknown GKZ: ${gkz} (${props.name})`);
   322→      continue;
   323→    }
   324→    if (!byKfz[kfz]) byKfz[kfz] = [];
   325→
   326→    const geom = feature.geometry;
   327→    if (geom.type === 'Polygon') {
   328→      byKfz[kfz].push(geom.coordinates);
   329→    } else if (geom.type === 'MultiPolygon') {
   330→      for (const polygon of geom.coordinates) {
   331→        byKfz[kfz].push(polygon);
   332→      }
   333→    }
   334→  }
   335→
   336→  console.log(`Mapped to ${Object.keys(byKfz).length} Bezirke`);
   337→
   338→  // Build output
   339→  const entries = {};
   340→  for (const [kfz, polygons] of Object.entries(byKfz)) {
   341→    const meta = BEZIRK_META[kfz];
   342→    if (!meta) {
   343→      console.warn(`  No metadata for KFZ: ${kfz}`);
   344→      continue;
   345→    }
   346→
   347→    // Merge all rings into a single SVG path
   348→    const allRings = [];
   349→    const allOuterRings = [];
   350→    for (const polygon of polygons) {
   351→      for (const ring of polygon) {
   352→        allRings.push(ring);
   353→      }
   354→      allOuterRings.push(polygon[0]); // outer ring for centroid
   355→    }
   356→
   357→    const d = coordsToSvgPath(allRings);
   358→
   359→    // Centroid from all outer rings combined
   360→    let cx = 0, cy = 0, n = 0;
   361→    for (const ring of allOuterRings) {
   362→      for (const [lon, lat] of ring) {
   363→        cx += projectLon(lon);
   364→        cy += projectLat(lat);
   365→        n++;
   366→      }
   367→    }
   368→    cx = Math.round(cx / n * 100) / 100;
   369→    cy = Math.round(cy / n * 100) / 100;
   370→
   371→    entries[kfz] = {
   372→      d,
   373→      labelX: cx,
   374→      labelY: cy,
   375→      name: meta.name,
   376→      bundesland_code: meta.bundesland_code,
   377→    };
   378→  }
   379→
   380→  // Generate TypeScript output
   381→  const lines = [
   382→    '// Auto-generated by scripts/generate-bezirk-paths.mjs',
   383→    '// Source: github.com/ginseng666/GeoJSON-TopoJSON-Austria (CC BY-SA 3.0)',
   384→    '// Do not edit manually.',
   385→    '',
   386→    'export const BEZIRK_PATHS: Record<string, {',
   387→    '  d: string; labelX: number; labelY: number;',
   388→    '  name: string; bundesland_code: string;',
   389→    '}> = {',
   390→  ];
   391→
   392→  const sortedKeys = Object.keys(entries).sort((a, b) => {
   393→    const ba = entries[a].bundesland_code;
   394→    const bb = entries[b].bundesland_code;
   395→    if (ba !== bb) return ba.localeCompare(bb);
   396→    return a.localeCompare(b);
   397→  });
   398→
   399→  for (const kfz of sortedKeys) {
   400→    const e = entries[kfz];
   401→    lines.push(`  '${kfz}': {`);
   402→    lines.push(`    d: '${e.d}',`);
   403→    lines.push(`    labelX: ${e.labelX}, labelY: ${e.labelY},`);
   404→    lines.push(`    name: '${e.name.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', bundesland_code: '${e.bundesland_code}',`);
   405→    lines.push(`  },`);
   406→  }
   407→
   408→  lines.push('};');
   409→  lines.push('');
   410→
   411→  // ─── Bundesländer borders (same projection) ───
   412→  const blEntries = {};
   413→  for (const feature of blGeojson.features) {
   414→    const props = feature.properties;
   415→    const iso = String(props.iso || props.id || '');
   416→    const dbCode = BL_ISO_TO_DB[iso];
   417→    if (!dbCode) {
   418→      console.warn(`  Unknown BL iso: ${iso} (${props.name})`);
   419→      continue;
   420→    }
   421→    const geom = feature.geometry;
   422→    const allRings = [];
   423→    if (geom.type === 'Polygon') {
   424→      for (const ring of geom.coordinates) allRings.push(ring);
   425→    } else if (geom.type === 'MultiPolygon') {
   426→      for (const polygon of geom.coordinates) {
   427→        for (const ring of polygon) allRings.push(ring);
   428→      }
   429→    }
   430→    blEntries[dbCode] = coordsToSvgPath(allRings);
   431→  }
   432→
   433→  lines.push('export const BL_BORDER_PATHS: Record<string, string> = {');
   434→  for (const code of Object.keys(blEntries).sort()) {
   435→    lines.push(`  '${code}': '${blEntries[code]}',`);
   436→  }
   437→  lines.push('};');
   438→  lines.push('');
   439→
   440→  const outPath = resolve(__dirname, '../client/src/data/bezirkPaths.ts');
   441→  writeFileSync(outPath, lines.join('\n'));
   442→  console.log(`Written ${sortedKeys.length} Bezirke + ${Object.keys(blEntries).length} BL borders to ${outPath}`);
   443→  console.log(`File size: ${(lines.join('\n').length / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`);
   444→}
   445→
   446→main().catch(console.error);
   447→

     1→import { useState } from 'react';
     2→import { BEZIRK_PATHS, BL_BORDER_PATHS } from '../data/bezirkPaths';
     3→
     4→interface BezirkData {
     5→  bezirk_code: string;
     6→  participants: number;
     7→  reports: number;
     8→}
     9→
    10→interface Props {
    11→  data: BezirkData[];
    12→}
    13→
    14→// BL_BORDER_PATHS imported from generated data (same projection as BEZIRK_PATHS)
    15→
    16→const BL_NAMES: Record<string, string> = {
    17→  '01': 'Wien', '02': 'Salzburg', '03': 'Niederösterreich', '04': 'Burgenland',
    18→  '05': 'Oberösterreich', '06': 'Steiermark', '07': 'Tirol', '08': 'Kärnten',
    19→  '09': 'Vorarlberg',
    20→};
    21→
    22→function getHeatColor(value: number, max: number): string {
    23→  if (max === 0 || value === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
    24→  const t = Math.min(value / max, 1);
    25→  // Green (120) → Yellow (60) → Red (0) via HSL
    26→  const h = 120 * (1 - t);
    27→  const s = 70 + 15 * t; // 70-85%
    28→  const l = 45 + 10 * (1 - t); // 45-55%
    29→  return `hsl(${h}, ${s}%, ${l}%)`;
    30→}
    31→
    32→export default function AustriaBezirkMap({ data }: Props) {
    33→  const [tooltip, setTooltip] = useState<{
    34→    x: number;
    35→    y: number;
    36→    code: string;
    37→    name: string;
    38→    bundesland: string;
    39→    participants: number;
    40→    reports: number;
    41→  } | null>(null);
    42→
    43→  const dataMap = new Map(data.map((d) => [d.bezirk_code, d]));
    44→  const maxParticipants = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.participants), 1);
    45→
    46→  const showTooltip = (
    47→    e: React.MouseEvent,
    48→    code: string,
    49→    labelX: number,
    50→    labelY: number,
    51→    participants: number,
    52→    reports: number,
    53→    name: string,
    54→    bundesland: string,
    55→  ) => {
    56→    const svg = e.currentTarget.closest('svg');
    57→    if (!svg) return;
    58→    const rect = svg.getBoundingClientRect();
    59→    const pt = svg.createSVGPoint();
    60→    pt.x = labelX;
    61→    pt.y = labelY;
    62→    const ctm = svg.getScreenCTM();
    63→    if (!ctm) return;
    64→    const screenPt = pt.matrixTransform(ctm);
    65→    setTooltip({
    66→      x: screenPt.x - rect.left,
    67→      y: screenPt.y - rect.top - 10,
    68→      code,
    69→      name,
    70→      bundesland,
    71→      participants,
    72→      reports,
    73→    });
    74→  };
    75→
    76→  return (
    77→    <div className="relative">
    78→      <svg
    79→        viewBox="-2 -51 629 470"
    80→        className="w-full h-auto max-h-[400px]"
    81→        preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
    82→        role="img"
    83→        aria-label="Bezirks-Heatmap von Österreich"
    84→      >
    85→        {/* Bezirk polygons */}
    86→        {Object.entries(BEZIRK_PATHS).map(([code, { d, labelX, labelY, name, bundesland_code }]) => {
    87→          const bz = dataMap.get(code);
    88→          const participants = bz?.participants ?? 0;
    89→          const reports = bz?.reports ?? 0;
    90→          const fill = getHeatColor(participants, maxParticipants);
    91→          const blName = BL_NAMES[bundesland_code] || '';
    92→
    93→          return (
    94→            <path
    95→              key={code}
    96→              d={d}
    97→              fill={fill}
    98→              stroke="#ffffff"
    99→              strokeWidth="0.5"
   100→              className="transition-opacity hover:opacity-75 cursor-pointer"
   101→              onMouseEnter={(e) => showTooltip(e, code, labelX, labelY, participants, reports, name, blName)}
   102→              onMouseLeave={() => setTooltip(null)}
   103→            />
   104→          );
   105→        })}
   106→
   107→        {/* Bundesland borders overlay */}
   108→        {Object.values(BL_BORDER_PATHS).map((d, i) => (
   109→          <path
   110→            key={`bl-${i}`}
   111→            d={d}
   112→            fill="none"
   113→            stroke="#1e3a5f"
   114→            strokeWidth="1.5"
   115→            pointerEvents="none"
   116→          />
   117→        ))}
   118→
   119→        {/* Labels for all Bezirke */}
   120→        {Object.entries(BEZIRK_PATHS).map(([code, { labelX, labelY }]) => {
   121→          const bz = dataMap.get(code);
   122→          const hasData = bz && bz.participants > 0;
   123→          return (
   124→            <text
   125→              key={`label-${code}`}
   126→              x={labelX}
   127→              y={labelY}
   128→              textAnchor="middle"
   129→              dominantBaseline="central"
   130→              fontSize={hasData ? '6' : '5'}
   131→              fontWeight={hasData ? 'bold' : 'normal'}
   132→              fill={hasData ? '#1e3a5f' : '#9ca3af'}
   133→              pointerEvents="none"
   134→              stroke="#ffffff"
   135→              strokeWidth={hasData ? '2' : '1.5'}
   136→              paintOrder="stroke"
   137→            >
   138→              {code}
   139→            </text>
   140→          );
   141→        })}
   142→      </svg>
   143→
   144→      {/* Tooltip */}
   145→      {tooltip && (
   146→        <div
   147→          className="absolute bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded px-2 py-1.5 pointer-events-none shadow-lg z-10 whitespace-nowrap"
   148→          style={{
   149→            left: tooltip.x,
   150→            top: tooltip.y,
   151→            transform: 'translate(-50%, -100%)',
   152→          }}
   153→        >
   154→          <div className="font-bold">{tooltip.code} — {tooltip.name}</div>
   155→          <div className="text-gray-300">{tooltip.bundesland}</div>
   156→          <div>Teilnehmer: {tooltip.participants}</div>
   157→          <div>Rapporte: {tooltip.reports}</div>
   158→        </div>
   159→      )}
   160→
   161→      {/* Legend */}
   162→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500">
   163→        <span>0</span>
   164→        <div
   165→          className="h-3 w-32 rounded"
   166→          style={{
   167→            background: 'linear-gradient(to right, hsl(120,70%,55%), hsl(60,78%,50%), hsl(0,85%,45%))',
   168→          }}
   169→        />
   170→        <span>{maxParticipants}</span>
   171→        <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400">Teilnehmer</span>
   172→      </div>
   173→    </div>
   174→  );
   175→}
   176→

> AGENT

Deployed successfully. The Bezirk heatmap now uses the correct Mercator projection, matching the existing Bundesland map's proportions. The key fix was:

- **Corrected Mercator Y calibration constants**: `SY_M` from -5721.8 to -6389.5, `OY_M` from 6493.0 to 6249.8 — the previous values were derived from incorrect hand calculations of the `mercatorY()` function
- **Coordinate range now matches existing map**: Y range is now -40 to 399 (matching Wien at y≈97), instead of the old 860-1253 which was completely off
- **Removed `max-h-[400px]`** constraint that was squishing the map

Excellent! Now I have the actual coordinates. Let me create a final summary report:

## Summary: Visual Difference Root Cause Analysis

Based on my thorough investigation of both `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/AustriaMap.tsx` and `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/AustriaBezirkMap.tsx` along with the generation script, here is the definitive analysis:

### **Finding: The Root Cause is NOT a Projection Mismatch**

While both maps claim to use the identical Mercator projection calibrated from Wien (550, 97) and Bregenz (30, 215), the visual difference stems from **three distinct but complementary causes**:

---

## 1. **Different Geodata Sources**

- **AustriaMap.tsx (Bundesland paths)**: Uses MapSVG data (CC BY 4.0), which provides pre-merged Bundesland geometries
- **AustriaBezirkMap.tsx**: Uses GeoJSON-TopoJSON-Austria (github.com/ginseng666/GeoJSON-TopoJSON-Austria, CC BY-SA 3.0)
  - Source: 2021 Austrian administrative boundaries
  - Simplified to 99.5% (line 17 of generate-bezirk-paths.mjs)

Even with identical projection math, different source data = different coordinates after projection.

---

## 2. **Geometry Reconstruction Mismatch**

**Key architectural difference:**

| AustriaMap (BL_PATHS) | AustriaBezirkMap (BEZIRK_PATHS + BL_BORDER_PATHS) |
|---|---|
| Single merged polygon per Bundesland (9 regions total) | Individual polygons per Bezirk (95 districts), then recomputed BL borders |
| Tirol = 1 path | Tirol = 9 paths (IC, IL, IM, KB, KU, LA, LZ, RE, SZ) |
| Kärnten = 1 path | Kärnten = 9 paths (FE, HE, KC, KL, SP, SV, VI, VL, VK, WO) |

**The Problem**: When you decompose a Bundesland into individual Bezirk boundaries and later reconstruct the BL border (BL_BORDER_PATHS), the union of all Bezirk boundaries may differ from the original merged polygon due to:
- Rounding errors across thousands of coordinate points
- Precision loss from 99.5% simplification
- Imperfect polygon unions not matching the original boundary exactly

**Evidence from Wien**:
- BL Wien labelX/Y: (550, 97) ← reference calibration point
- Bezirk W labelX/Y: (549.05, 97.98) ← nearly identical (good!)
- BUT: BL Wien path starts at M550.84, 84.85
- Bezirk W path starts at M543.38, 90.02
- **Difference: X = -7.46, Y = +5.17 pixels** ← This is the geometry itself, not labeling!

---

## 3. **Aspect Ratio Amplification**

The visual mismatch is amplified by different SVG viewport configurations:

**AustriaMap.tsx (line 194)**:
```
viewBox="-300 -120 1100 560"
```
- Includes neighbor countries (negative X coordinates)
- Total width: 1100 units
- Shows context beyond Austria

**AustriaBezirkMap.tsx (line 79)**:
```
viewBox="0 -50 625 460"
```
- Tight framing around Austria only
- Total width: 625 units (57% of BL map width!)
- No neighbor countries shown

**Result**: A 7-pixel X offset in absolute terms becomes ~12% visual shift when the entire map is 57% smaller.

---

## 4. **Rounding & Simplification Cascades**

From generate-bezirk-paths.mjs:
- Line 267: Coordinates rounded to 2 decimal places: `Math.round(x * 100) / 100`
- 95 Bezirke × thousands of points = cumulative rounding errors
- 99.5% GeoJSON simplification removes "unnecessary" detail
  - Particularly impacts small districts and precise boundaries
  - Can alter district centroid positions (labelX/Y calculations)

---

## Summary Comparison Table

| Aspect | AustriaMap BL | AustriaBezirkMap Bezirk |
|--------|---------------|--------------------------|
| **Geodata Source** | MapSVG (CC BY 4.0) | GeoJSON-TopoJSON (2021, 99.5% simplified) |
| **Geometry Structure** | 1 polygon per BL | 95 separate polygons per Bezirk |
| **Projection Formula** | Mercator (calibrated W/B) | Mercator (calibrated W/B) |
| **Calibration Points** | Wien (550,97), Bregenz (30,215) | Wien (550,97), Bregenz (30,215) |
| **Label Position (Wien)** | (550, 97) | (549.05, 97.98) |
| **Path Geometry (Wien)** | M550.84, 84.85... | M543.38, 90.02... |
| **ViewBox** | -300 -120 1100 560 | 0 -50 625 460 |
| **Austria-only X range** | 30–600 (570 units) | 0–625 (625 units) |

---

## Conclusion

**The maps are NOT using mismatched projections.** Both correctly apply Mercator projection with identical calibration constants. The visual difference is caused by:

1. **Fundamental data difference** (MapSVG vs GeoJSON-TopoJSON-Austria)
2. **Architectural difference** (merged BL polygons vs decomposed Bezirk polygons)
3. **Simplification differences** (unknown for BL_PATHS vs 99.5% for Bezirk)
4. **Viewport/aspect ratio differences** (wider vs tighter framing)

The geometry coordinates differ by ~5-7 pixels in absolute terms, which becomes visually significant when the viewport is tightened by 43% (from 1100 to 625 units width).

**To fix the visual mismatch**, you would need to either:
- Use the same source geodata for both (re-generate BL_PATHS from GeoJSON-TopoJSON-Austria)
- Or regenerate Bezirk paths using MapSVG source data
- Plus harmonize the viewBox dimensions and aspect ratios

  "command": "nix-shell -p nodejs --run \"node -e \\\"\n// Extract specific reference points from existing BL_PATHS\n// Check what Y scale the existing map actually uses\nconst fs = require('fs');\nconst content = fs.readFileSync('client/src/components/AustriaMap.tsx', 'utf8');\n\n// Wien (OE1, code 01) - northernmost point and southernmost\n// Kärnten (OE8, code 08) - southernmost BL\n// Let's get ALL Austrian BL coordinates\nconst blPaths = {};\nconst blRegex = /'(0[1-9])':\\s*\\\\{[^}]*d:\\s*'([^']+)'/g;\nlet m;\nwhile ((m = blRegex.exec(content)) !== null) {\n  blPaths[m[1]] = m[2];\n}\n\n// Find extreme Y points per BL\nfor (const [code, d] of Object.entries(blPaths)) {\n  const coordRe = /[ML]([-\\\\d.]+),([-\\\\d.]+)/g;\n  let c;\n  let ys = [];\n  while ((c = coordRe.exec(d)) !== null) {\n    ys.push(parseFloat(c[2]));\n  }\n  console.log('BL', code, '- Y min:', Math.min(...ys).toFixed(1), 'max:', Math.max(...ys).toFixed(1));\n}\n\n// Now check: Wien center is at y≈97, Kärnten (Villach) should be at the bottom\n// In the existing map, the Y range for Austria is 0.7 to 313.5\n// In our bezirk map, it's -39.8 to 398.7\n// Width is the same (~600). So the Y scale is different.\n\n// Let's compute what SY the existing map uses (linear Y):\n// Wien: lat=48.21, y=97\n// Kärnten bottom: lat≈46.37 (Karawanken), should be at max Y\n// If linear: SY = (y2-y1)/(lat1-lat2) = (313-97)/(48.21-46.37) = 216/1.84 = 117.4\n// X scale: SX = 78.55 per degree\n\n// For Mercator: SY should be SX/cos(lat) = 78.55/cos(47.5°) = 78.55/0.676 = 116.2\n// But our calibrated SY_M gives much larger Y range...\n\n// The existing map might just be using LINEAR Y (equirectangular)!\n// Linear: Y_per_degree ≈ 117 (from above)\n// X_per_degree = 78.55\n// Ratio: 117/78.55 = 1.49 ≈ 1/cos(47.5°) = 1.48\n\n// That's exactly what equirectangular with cos(lat) correction looks like!\n// But Mercator at these latitudes gives MORE Y stretching.\n\nconsole.log();\nconsole.log('--- Projection analysis ---');\nconsole.log('X scale (from Wien-Bregenz): 78.55 px/degree');\nconsole.log('Existing map Y range for Austria: 0.7 to 313.5 = 312.8 px');\nconsole.log('Austria lat range: ~46.37 to ~49.02 = 2.65 degrees');\nconsole.log('Existing Y scale: ' + (312.8/2.65).toFixed(1) + ' px/degree');\nconsole.log('Ratio Y/X: ' + (312.8/2.65/78.55).toFixed(3));\nconsole.log('1/cos(47.5): ' + (1/Math.cos(47.5*Math.PI/180)).toFixed(3));\n\n// Mercator Y gives:\nfunction mercatorY(latDeg) {\n  const latRad = latDeg * Math.PI / 180;\n  return Math.log(Math.tan(Math.PI / 4 + latRad / 2));\n}\nconst mTop = mercatorY(49.02);\nconst mBot = mercatorY(46.37);\nconsole.log('Mercator Y range: ' + mTop.toFixed(4) + ' to ' + mBot.toFixed(4) + ' = ' + (mTop-mBot).toFixed(4));\nconsole.log('If using our SY_M=-6389.5: height = ' + (Math.abs(-6389.5 * (mTop - mBot))).toFixed(1) + ' px');\nconsole.log('Actual height in existing map: 312.8 px');\nconsole.log('Needed SY_M for existing map: ' + (312.8 / (mBot - mTop)).toFixed(1));\n\\\"\"",
  "description": "Analyze projection type of existing map"
}

Found it. The existing BL map uses **linear Y** (not Mercator), with Y scale ≈ 118 px/degree. Our bezirk map uses Mercator Y which stretches height 1.4x at Austrian latitudes. That's why it looks stretched.

Let me fix the generation script to use the same linear projection as the existing map.

> AGENT

Deployed. The root cause was: the existing BL map uses **linear Y projection** (equirectangular), not Mercator. Mercator stretches Y by 1/cos(lat) which at 48°N means 1.4x more height. Now both maps use the same linear projection and the proportions match:

- Bezirk map: 599 x 312 (ratio 1.92:1)
- BL map: 612 x 313 (ratio 1.96:1)
